{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01PKEensNfW364zgqNHA5XxK","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→```csharp\n     2→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n     3→//  Project : PulseOps Warehouse — Serverless Productivity Intelligence Fabric\n     4→//  File    : module_10.txt   (implementation only; filename is controlled by build pipeline)\n     5→//  License : MIT\n     6→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n     7→//  Description\n     8→//  ------------\n     9→//  Transformation module that materialises productivity-oriented KPIs from a heterogenous event feed.\n    10→//  Implements the Strategy-Pattern so that additional KPIs can be plugged in without redeploying the\n    11→//  compute units.  The module is designed to execute inside an AWS Lambda but is consciously decoupled\n    12→//  from the hosting environment (no direct dependency on Amazon.Lambda.* packages) so it can also be\n    13→//  reused by Glue, Fargate, or on-prem batch jobs.\n    14→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    15→\n    16→using System;\n    17→using System.Collections.Concurrent;\n    18→using System.Collections.Generic;\n    19→using System.Diagnostics;\n    20→using System.Linq;\n    21→using System.Threading;\n    22→using System.Threading.Tasks;\n    23→\n    24→using Amazon.S3;\n    25→using Amazon.S3.Model;\n    26→\n    27→using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;\n    28→\n    29→namespace PulseOps.Warehouse.Transformations\n    30→{\n    31→    #region Public DTOs / Contracts -----------------------------------------------------------------\n    32→\n    33→    /// <summary>\n    34→    /// Thin wrapper around any raw event that enters the warehouse.\n    35→    /// Envelopes allow us to standardise metadata required for transformation,\n    36→    /// without rewriting every single ingestion lambda when an attribute is added later on.\n    37→    /// </summary>\n    38→    /// <remarks>\n    39→    /// The schema mirrors the one emitted by the <c>EventIngressProcessor</c> lambda.\n    40→    /// </remarks>\n    41→    public sealed record EventEnvelope\n    42→    {\n    43→        public required string EventId          { get; Strategies may buffer events\n    76→        /// internally if the KPI spans a sliding window (e.g., Deployment Lead-Time).\n    77→        /// </summary>\n    78→        ValueTask<IEnumerable<KpiCalculationResult>> ProcessAsync(\n    79→            EventEnvelope envelope,\n    80→            CancellationToken                        ct = default);\n    81→    }\n    82→\n    83→    #endregion\n    84→\n    85→    #region Transformation Engine -------------------------------------------------------------------\n    86→\n    87→    /// <summary>\n    88→    /// Coordinates incoming event stream and dispatches to registered KPI strategies.\n    89→    /// Takes care of parallelism, back-pressure, exception isolation, and durability (S3 write).\n    90→    /// </summary>\n    91→    public sealed class KpiTransformationEngine : IAsyncDisposable\n    92→    {\n    93→        private readonly IReadOnlyList<IKpiCalculationStrategy> _strategies;\n    94→        private readonly IS3KpiWriter                           _writer;\n    95→        private readonly ILogger<KpiTransformationEngine>       _logger;\n    96→        private readonly SemaphoreSlim                          _parallelism;\n    97→\n    98→        public KpiTransformationEngine(\n    99→            IEnumerable<IKpiCalculationStrategy> strategies,\n   100→            IS3KpiWriter                           writer,\n   101→            ILogger<KpiTransformationEngine>       logger,\n   102→            int                                    maxDegreeOfParallelism = 8)\n   103→        {\n   104→            _strategies   = strategies?.ToList() ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(strategies));\n   105→            _writer       = writer     ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(writer));\n   106→            _logger       = logger     ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(logger));\n   107→            if (!_strategies.Any())\n   108→                throw new ArgumentException(\"At least one KPI strategy must be provided.\", nameof(strategies));\n   109→\n   110→            _parallelism  = new SemaphoreSlim(maxDegreeOfParallelism, maxDegreeOfParallelism);\n   111→        }\n   112→\n   113→        /// <summary>\n   114→        /// Orchestrates transformation for a batch of events.\n   115→        /// </summary>\n   116→        public async ValueTask ExecuteAsync(\n   117→            IEnumerable<EventEnvelope> events,\n   118→            CancellationToken         ct = default)\n   119→        {\n   120→            ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(events);\n   121→\n   122→            var tasks = new List<Task>();\n   123→\n   124→            foreach (var envelope in events)\n   125→            {\n   126→                await _parallelism.WaitAsync(ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n   127→\n   128→                // Fire-and-forget – safe because we await all tasks in the list below\n   129→                tasks.Add(Task.Run(async () =>\n   130→                {\n   131→                    try\n   132→                    {\n   133→                        await DispatchToStrategiesAsync(envelope, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n   134→                    }\n   135→                    catch (Exception ex)\n   136→                    {\n   137→                        _logger.LogError(ex,\n   138→                            \"Uncaught exception while processing event {EventId}. \" +\n   139→                            \"The event will be re-queued to the DLQ by the Lambda runtime.\",\n   140→                            envelope.EventId);\n   141→                        // Bubble up to trigger Lambda DLQ – do NOT swallow\n   142→                        throw;\n   143→                    }\n   144→                    finally\n   145→                    {\n   146→                        _parallelism.Release();\n   147→                    }\n   148→                }, ct));\n   149→            }\n   150→\n   151→            await Task.WhenAll(tasks).ConfigureAwait(false);\n   152→        }\n   153→\n   154→        private async Task DispatchToStrategiesAsync(EventEnvelope envelope, CancellationToken ct)\n   155→        {\n   156→            var sw = Stopwatch.StartNew();\n   157→\n   158→            var interestedStrategies = _strategies.Where(s => s.CanHandle(envelope)).ToArray();\n   159→            if (interestedStrategies.Length == 0)\n   160→            {\n   161→                _logger.LogDebug(\"No KPI strategy registered for event {EventType}.\", envelope.EventType);\n   162→                return;\n   163→            }\n   164→\n   165→            var kpiRows = new ConcurrentBag<KpiCalculationResult>();\n   166→\n   167→            // Parallel but limited to #strategies so trivial overhead\n   168→            var strategyTasks = interestedStrategies.Select(async strategy =>\n   169→            {\n   170→                try\n   171→                {\n   172→                    var rows = await strategy.ProcessAsync(envelope, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n   173→                    foreach (var row in rows) \"Strategy {KpiStrategy} failed while processing EventId={EventId}. Skipping strategy.\",\n   179→                        strategy.KpiName, envelope.EventId);\n   180→                }\n   181→            });\n   182→\n   183→            await Task.WhenAll(strategyTasks).ConfigureAwait(false);\n   184→\n   185→            // Commit results to the Data Lake (S3, parquet files partitioned by YYYY/MM/DD)\n   186→            if (kpiRows.Count > 0)\n   187→            {\n   188→                await _writer.WriteAsync(kpiRows, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n   189→            }\n   190→\n   191→            _logger.LogInformation(\n   192→                \"Processed EventId={EventId} in {ElapsedMs:n0} ms with {RowCount} KPI rows.\",\n   193→                envelope.EventId, sw.ElapsedMilliseconds, kpiRows.Count);\n   194→        }\n   195→\n   196→        public async ValueTask DisposeAsync()\n   197→        {\n   198→            _parallelism?.Dispose();\n   199→            if (_writer is IAsyncDisposable asyncDisposable)\n   200→                await asyncDisposable.DisposeAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);\n   201→        }\n   202→    }\n   203→\n   204→    #endregion\n   205→\n   206→    #region Strategy Implementations ----------------------------------------------------------------\n   207→\n   208→    /// <summary>\n   209→    /// Calculates the ratio between focus time and total working hours on a per-user, per-day basis.\n   210→    /// Source events:\n   211→    ///   - CalendarEventCreated / Updated\n   212→    ///   - WorkSessionStarted / Ended (emitted by IDE plugins, CLI wrappers, browser extensions, …)\n   213→    /// </summary>\n   214→    internal sealed class FocusTimeRatioStrategy : IKpiCalculationStrategy\n   215→    {\n   216→        private const string CalendarEventType = \"CalendarEvent\";\n   217→        private const string WorkSessionType   = \"WorkSession\";\n   218→\n   219→        public string KpiName => \"focus_time_ratio\";\n   220→\n   221→        public bool CanHandle(EventEnvelope envelope)\n   222→            => envelope.EventType is CalendarEventType or WorkSessionType;\n   223→\n   224→        public async ValueTask<IEnumerable<KpiCalculationResult>> ProcessAsync(\n   225→            EventEnvelope   envelope,\n   226→            CancellationToken ct = default)\n   227→        {\n   228→            // NOTE: In a real-world scenario, we would use a distributed cache (e.g., DAX or Redis)\n   229→            //       to store partial aggregates.  For brevity, this implementation simply returns\n   230→            //       an immediate KPI row when sufficient information is present on the event itself.\n   231→\n   232→            if (envelope.EventType == WorkSessionType &&\n   233→                envelope.Metadata.TryGetValue(\"DurationMinutes\", out var durationStr) &&\n   234→                int.TryParse(durationStr, out var durationMinutes))\n   235→            {\n   236→                var userId = envelope.Metadata[\"UserId\"]!;\n   237→                var date   = envelope.Created.UtcDateTime.Date;\n   238→\n   239→                var result = new KpiCalculationResult\n   240→                {\n   241→                    KpiName        = KpiName,\n   242→                    WindowStartUtc = date,\n   243→                    WindowEndUtc   = date.AddDays(1),\n   244→                    Value          = durationMinutes, // later aggregated to obtain total focus time\n   245→                    Dimensions = new Dictionary<string, string>\n   246→                    {\n   247→                        [\"user_id\"] = userId,\n   248→                        [\"metric\"]  = \"focus_minutes\"\n   249→                    }\n   250→                };\n   251→\n   252→                return new[] { result };\n   253→            }\n   254→\n   255→            if (envelope.EventType == CalendarEventType &&\n   256→                envelope.Metadata.TryGetValue(\"DurationMinutes\", out var calendarDurationStr) &&\n   257→                int.TryParse(calendarDurationStr, out var meetingMinutes))\n   258→            {\n   259→                var userId = envelope.Metadata[\"OrganizerId\"]!;\n   260→                var date   = envelope.Created.UtcDateTime.Date;\n   261→\n   262→                var result = new KpiCalculationResult\n   263→                {\n   264→                    KpiName        = KpiName,\n   265→                    WindowStartUtc = date,\n   266→                    WindowEndUtc   = date.AddDays(1),\n   267→                    Value          = meetingMinutes,\n   268→                    Dimensions = new Dictionary<string, string>\n   269→                    {\n   270→                        [\"user_id\"] = userId,\n   271→                        [\"metric\"]  = \"meeting_minutes\"\n   272→                    }\n   273→                };\n   274→\n   275→                return new[] { result };\n   276→            }\n   277→\n   278→            return Array.Empty<KpiCalculationResult>();\n   279→        }\n   280→    }\n   281→\n   282→    /// <summary>\n   283→    /// Computes deployment lead-time (commit → production) on a per-repo basis.\n   284→    /// Maintains a small in-memory buffer which is flushed every <see cref=\"FlushInterval\"/>.\n   285→    /// </summary>\n   286→    internal sealed class DeploymentLeadTimeStrategy : IKpiCalculationStrategy, IDisposable\n   287→    {\n   288→        private const string CommitEventType     = \"CodeCommitPushed\";\n   289→        private const string ProdDeployEventType = \"CdPipelineDeployed\";\n   290→\n   291→        private readonly TimeSpan                               FlushInterval     = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5);\n   292→        private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, DateTime> _commitTimestamps = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);\n   293→        private readonly Timer                                  _flushTimer;\n   294→        private readonly ILogger                                _logger;\n   295→\n   296→        public string KpiName => \"deployment_lead_time\";\n   297→\n   298→        public DeploymentLeadTimeStrategy(ILogger<DeploymentLeadTimeStrategy> logger)\n   299→        {\n   300→            _logger      = logger;\n   301→            _flushTimer  = new Timer(_ => FlushExpired(), null, FlushInterval, FlushInterval);\n   302→        }\n   303→\n   304→        public bool CanHandle(EventEnvelope envelope)\n   305→            => envelope.EventType is CommitEventType or ProdDeployEventType;\n   306→\n   307→        public async ValueTask<IEnumerable<KpiCalculationResult>> ProcessAsync(\n   308→            EventEnvelope envelope,\n   309→            CancellationToken ct = default)\n   310→        {\n   311→            switch (envelope.EventType)\n   312→            {\n   313→                case CommitEventType:\n   314→                    {\n   315→                        var commitSha  = envelope.Metadata[\"CommitSha\"]!;\n   316→                        _commitTimestamps[commitSha] = envelope.Created.UtcDateTime;\n   317→                        break;\n   318→                    }\n   319→\n   320→                case ProdDeployEventType:\n   321→                    {\n   322→                        var commitSha = envelope.Metadata[\"CommitSha\"]!;\n   323→                        if (_commitTimestamps.TryRemove(commitSha, out var commitTime))\n   324→                        {\n   325→                            var leadTimeMinutes = (envelope.Created.UtcDateTime - commitTime).TotalMinutes;\n   326→                            var repo            = envelope.Metadata[\"Repository\"]!;\n   327→\n   328→                            var kpiRow = new KpiCalculationResult\n   329→                            {\n   330→                                KpiName        = KpiName,\n   331→                                WindowStartUtc = commitTime,\n   332→                                WindowEndUtc   = envelope.Created.UtcDateTime,\n   333→                                Value          = (decimal)leadTimeMinutes,\n   334→                                Dimensions = new Dictionary<string, string>\n   335→                                {\n   336→                                    [\"repository\"] = repo\n   337→                                }\n   338→                            };\n   339→                            return new[] { kpiRow };\n   340→                        }\n   341→        /// <summary>\n        /// Orchestrates transformation for a batch of events.\n        /// </summary>\n        public async ValueTask ExecuteAsync(\n            IEnumerable<EventEnvelope> envelope in events)\n            {\n                await _parallelism.WaitAsync(ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n                // Fire-and-forget – safe because we await all tasks in the list below\n _parallelism.Release();\n                    }\n                }, ct));\n            }\n\n            await Task.WhenAll(tasks).ConfigureAwait(false);\n        }\n\n        private async Task DispatchToStrategiesAsync(EventEnvelope envelope, CancellationToken ct)\n        {\n            var sw = Stopwatch.StartNew();\n\n            var interestedStrategies = _strategies.Where(s => s.CanHandle(envelope)).ToArray();\n            if (interestedStrategies.Length == 0)\n            {\n                _logger.LogDebug(\"No KPI strategy registered for event {EventType}.\", envelope.EventType);\n                return;\n            }\n\n            var kpiRows = new ConcurrentBag<KpiCalculationResult>();\n\n            // Parallel but limited to #strategies so trivial overhead\n            var strategyTasks = interestedStrategies.Select(async strategy =>\n            {\n                try\n                {\n                    var rows = await strategy.ProcessAsync(envelope, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                    foreach (var row in rows) kpiRows.Add(row);\n                }\n                catch (Exception ex)\n                {\n                    _logger.LogWarning(ex,\n                        \"Strategy {KpiStrategy} failed while processing EventId={EventId}. Skipping strategy.\",\n                        strategy.KpiName, envelope.EventId);\n                }\n            });\n\n            await Task.WhenAll(strategyTasks).ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n            // Commit results to the Data Lake (S3, parquet files partitioned by YYYY/MM/DD)\n            if (kpiRows.Count > 0)\n            {\n                await _writer.WriteAsync(kpiRows, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n            }\n\n            _logger.LogInformation(\n                \"Processed EventId={EventId} in {ElapsedMs:n0} ms with {RowCount} KPI rows.\",\n                envelope.EventId, sw.ElapsedMilliseconds, kpiRows.Count);\n        }\n\n        public async ValueTask DisposeAsync()\n        {\n            _parallelism?.Dispose();\n            if (_writer is IAsyncDisposable asyncDisposable)\n                await asyncDisposable.DisposeAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);\n        }\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n    #region Strategy Implementations ----------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Calculates the ratio between focus time and total working hours on a per-user, per-day basis.\n    /// Source events:\n    ///   -           var userId = envelope.Metadata[\"OrganizerId\"]!;\n                var date   = envelope.Created.UtcDateTime.Date;\n\n                var result = new KpiCalculationResult\n                {\n                    KpiName        = KpiName,\n                    WindowStartUtc = date,\n                    WindowEndUtc   = date.AddDays(1),\n                    Value          = meetingMinutes,\n                    Dimensions = new Dictionary<string, string>\n                    {\n                        [\"user_id\"] = userId,\n                        [\"metric\"]  = \"meeting_minutes\"\n                    }\n                };\n\n                return new[] { result };\n            }\n\n            return Array.Empty<KpiCalculationResult>();\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Computes deployment lead-time (commit → production) on a per-repo basis.\n    /// Maintains a small in-memory buffer which is flushed every <see cref=\"FlushInterval\"/>.\n    /// </summary>\n    internal sealed class DeploymentLeadTimeStrategy : IKpiCalculationStrategy, IDisposable\n    {\n        private const string CommitEventType     = \"CodeCommitPushed\";\n        private const string ProdDeployEventType = \"CdPipelineDeployed\";\n\n        private readonly TimeSpan                               FlushInterval     = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5);\n        private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, DateTime> _commitTimestamps = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);\n        private readonly Timer                                  _flushTimer;\n        private readonly ILogger                                _logger;\n\n        public string KpiName => \"deployment_lead_time\";\n\n        public      {\n                case CommitEventType:\n                    {\n                        var commitSha  = envelope.Metadata[\"CommitSha\"]!;\n                        _commitTimestamps[commitSha] return new[] { kpiRow };\n                        }\n                        break;\n                    }\n            }\n\n            return Array.Empty<KpiCalculationResult>();\n        }\n\n        private void FlushExpired()\n        {\n            var threshold = DateTime.UtcNow.AddHours(-24); // assume deploy happens within 24 h\n            foreach (var kvp in _commitTimestamps)\n            {\n                if (kvp.Value < threshold && _commitTimestamps.TryRemove(kvp.Key, out _))\n                {\n                    _logger.LogDebug(\n                        \"Dropped stale commit     = s3     ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(s3));\n            _bucket = bucket ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(bucket));\n            _logger = logger ?? throw new default)\n        {\n            ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(rows);\n\n            var nowUtc     = DateTime.UtcNow;\n            var partition  = $\"{nowUtc:yyyy/MM/dd}\";\n            var              ContentBody = jsonLines,\n                ContentType = \"application/json\"\n            };\n\n            await RetryPolicy.ExecuteAsync(\n                async () => await }\n\n        private static TimeSpan Percentile(IReadOnlyList<TimeSpan> data, double percentile)\n        {\n            if (data.Count == 0) return TimeSpan.Zero;\n\n            var ordered = data.OrderBy(x => x).ToArray();\n            var index   = (int)Math.Ceiling(percentile * ordered.Length) - 1;\n            index       = Math.Clamp(index, 0, ordered.Length - 1);\n\n            return ordered[index];\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Calculates Meeting Cost Index – total salary cost of meetings held in the timeframe.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed class MeetingCostIndexStrategy : IKpiComputationStrategy\n    {\n        public string StrategyId => \"meeting-cost-index\";\n\n        public async Task<KpiComputationResult> ComputeAsync(\n            KpiComputationContext context,\n            ILogger logger,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)\n        {\n            await Task.Yield();\n\n            var hourlyRate      = context.Parameters.TryGetValue(\"hourlyRateUSD\", out var rateElm) &&\n                                  rateElm.TryGetDecimal(out var rateUsd) ? rateUsd : 90m;\n\n            decimal totalCost = 0m;\n            int     meetings  = 0;\n\n            foreach (var meeting in context.RawEvents.OfType<MeetingEvent>())\n            {\n                cancellationToken.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();\n\n                var durationHours = meeting.Duration.TotalHours;\n                var attendeeCount = meeting.Attendees.Count;\n\n                totalCost += (decimal)durationHours * attendeeCount * hourlyRate;\n                meetings++;\n            }\n\n            var payloadJson = JsonSerializer.SerializeToElement(new\n            {\n                totalCostUSD = Math.Round(totalCost, 2),\n                meetingCount = meetings,\n                avgCostPerMeetingUSD = meetings == 0 ? 0 : Math.Round(totalCost / meetings, 2)\n            });\n\n            return new KpiComputationResult(\n                context.TenantId,\n                \"meeting_cost_index\",\n                context.From,\n                context.To,\n                payloadJson);\n        }\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n    #region 🧱 Supporting Domain Objects\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Simplistic in-memory buffer for raw events.    /// <summary>\n    /// Strategy that emits raw focus-time minutes for Focus activities.\n    /// Additional ratio aggregation is done downstream in Athena/QuickSight.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed class FocusTimeStrategy : IMetricComputationStrategy\n    {\n        private static readonly MetricDataPoint[] Empty = Array.Empty<MetricDataPoint>();\n\n        public bool CanHandle(in WorkActivityEvent workEvent)\n            => workEvent.Activity == ActivityType.Focus;\n\n        public ValueTask<IReadOnlyCollection<MetricDataPoint>> ComputeAsync(\n            WorkActivityEvent workEvent,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)\n        {\n            double minutes = workEvent.Duration.TotalMinutes;\n\n            if (minutes <= 0)\n            {\n                return ValueTask.FromResult<IReadOnlyCollection<MetricDataPoint>>(Empty);\n            }\n\n            var dp = new MetricDataPoint(\n                workEvent.UserId,\n                MetricName: \"focus_time_minutes\",\n                Value: Math.Round(minutes, 2, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero),\n                Timestamp: workEvent.EndUtc,\n                Dimensions: new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.Ordinal)\n                {\n                    [\"activity_type\"] = workEvent.Activity.ToString()\n                });\n\n            return ValueTask.FromResult<IReadOnlyCollection<MetricDataPoint>>(\n                new[] { dp });\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Strategy that calculates the total cost of a meeting based on its\n    /// duration and participants’ assumed hourly rate injected through metadata.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed class MeetingCostIndexStrategy : IMetricComputationStrategy\n    {\n        private static readonly MetricDataPoint[] Empty = Array.Empty<MetricDataPoint>();\n\n        private readonly double _defaultHourlyRateUsd;\n\n        public MeetingCostIndexStrategy(double defaultHourlyRateUsd = 75)\n        {\n            if (defaultHourlyRateUsd <= 0)\n                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(\n                    nameof(defaultHourlyRateUsd),\n                    \"Hourly rate must be positive.\");\n\n            _defaultHourlyRateUsd = defaultHourlyRateUsd;\n        }\n\n        public bool CanHandle(in WorkActivityEvent workEvent)\n            => CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)\n        {\n            // Guard against malformed events early; no metrics emitted.\n            if (workEvent.Duration <= TimeSpan.Zero)\n                return Empty;\n\n            // Extract meeting metadata (participants, rate override, etc.)\n            int participantCount = 0;\n            double? hourlyRateOverride = null;\n\n            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(workEvent.Metadata))\n            {\n                try\n                {\n                    var meetingMeta = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<MeetingMetadata>(\n                        workEvent.Metadata,\n                        SerializerOptions.Default);\n\n                    if (meetingMeta is not null)\n                    {\n                        participantCount   = meetingMeta.ParticipantCount ?? participantCount;\n                        hourlyRateOverride = meetingMeta.HourlyRateUsd;\n                    }\n                }\n                catch (JsonException ex)\n                {\n                    Log.Warning(\n                        ex,\n                        \"Failed to deserialize meeting metadata for event {EventId}. Raw: {Raw}\",\n                        workEvent.GetHashCode(),\n                        workEvent.Metadata);\n                }\n            }\n\n            participantCount = Math.Max(participantCount, 1); // At least one person.\n            double hourlyRate = hourlyRateOverride ?? _defaultHourlyRateUsd;\n\n            double costUsd = participantCount *\n                             hourlyRate *\n                             workEvent.Duration.TotalHours;\n\n            var dp = new MetricDataPoint(\n                workEvent.UserId,\n                MetricName: \"meeting_cost_usd\",\n                Value: Math.Round(costUsd, 2, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero),\n                Timestamp: workEvent.EndUtc,\n                Dimensions: new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.Ordinal)\n                {\n                    [\"participant_count\"] = participantCount.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),\n                    [\"hourly_rate\"]       = hourlyRate.ToString(\"F2\", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)\n                });\n\n            return new[] { dp };\n        }\n\n        // Internal DTO to map metadata JSON.\n        private sealed record MeetingMetadata\n        {\n            [JsonPropertyName(\"participantCount\")]\n            public int?   ParticipantCount { get; init; }\n\n            [JsonPropertyName(\"hourlyRateUsd\")]\n            public double? HourlyRateUsd   { get; init; }\n        }\n\n        // Re-usable serializer options.\n        private static class SerializerOptions\n        {\n            public static readonly    = JsonCommentHandling.Skip\n            };\n        }\n    }\n\n    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    //  Utilities\n    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Registry for fast lookup of the first matching strategy.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed class StrategyRegistry\n    {\n        private readonly ImmutableArray<IMetricComputationStrategy> _strategies;\n\n        public StrategyRegistry(IEnumerable<IMetricComputationStrategy> strategies)\n        {\n            _strategies = strategies is ImmutableArray<IMetricComputationStrategy> arr\n                        ? arr\n                        : strategies.ToImmutableArray();\n\n            if (_strategies.IsDefaultOrEmpty)\n                throw new ArgumentException(\n                    \"Strategy collection cannot be empty.\",\n                    nameof(strategies));\n        }\n\n        public bool TryResolve(\n            WorkActivityEvent            workEvent,\n            [NotNullWhen(true)]\n            out IMetricComputationStrategy? strategy)\n        {\n        }\n            }\n\n            strategy = null;\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// In-memory metric sink (primarily for unit tests and local return Task.CompletedTask;\n        }\n\n        public IReadOnlyList<MetricDataPoint> Snapshot()\n            => _buffer.ToImmutableArray();\n    }\n\n    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    public sealed class EventTransformationPipeline : IAsyncDisposable\n    {\n        private readonly BufferBlock<WorkActivityEvent>                _input;\n        private readonly TransformManyBlock<WorkActivityEvent, MetricDataPoint> _compute;\n        private readonly ActionBlock<MetricDataPoint>                  _sink;\n        private readonly ILogger                                       _log = Log.ForContext<EventTransformationPipeline>();\n        private readonly CancellationTokenSource                        _cts = new();\n\n        public EventTransformationPipeline(\n            StrategyRegistry strategyRegistry,\n            IMetricSink      metricSink,\n            int              maxDegreeOfParallelism = 4,\n            int              boundedCapacity        = 1_024)\n        {\n            ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(strategyRegistry);\n            ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(metricSink);\n\n            // Configure blocks\n            _input = new BufferBlock<WorkActivityEvent>(\n                new DataflowBlockOptions\n                {\n                    BoundedCapacity = boundedCapacity\n                });\n\n            _compute = new TransformManyBlock<WorkActivityEvent, MetricDataPoint>(\n                async (workEvent)             return await strategy.ComputeAsync(\n                                workEvent,\n                                _cts.Token);\n                        }\n\n             {\n                        _log.Error(\n                            ex,\n                            \"Metric computation failed for event {@Event}\",\n                            workEvent);\n                        return Array.Empty<MetricDataPoint>();\n                    }\n                },\n                new ExecutionDataflowBlockOptions\n                {\n                    MaxDegreeOfParallelism = maxDegreeOfParallelism,\n                    BoundedCapacity        = boundedCapacity,\n                    CancellationToken      = _cts.Token,\n         {\n                        await metricSink.WriteAsync(\n                            new[] { metric },\n                            _cts.Token);\n                    }\n          metric);\n                        // In production, failed metrics would be forwarded to a DLQ.\n                    }\n                },\n                new ExecutionDataflowBlockOptions\n                {\n                    MaxDegreeOfParallelism = maxDegreeOfParallelism,\n                    BoundedCapacity        = boundedCapacity,\n                    CancellationToken      = _cts.Token,\n                    EnsureOrdered          = false\n                });\n\n            // Wire-up blocks\n            _input.LinkTo(_compute,                    new DataflowLinkOptions { PropagateCompletion = true });\n            _compute.LinkTo(_sink,                     new DataflowLinkOptions { PropagateCompletion = true });\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Enqueues a new event for processing.  Respects back-pressure.\n        /// </summary>\n        public async Task SubmitAsync(\n            WorkActivityEvent  workEvent,\n            CancellationToken  cancellationToken = default)\n        {\n            if (!_input.Post(workEvent))\n            {\n                await _input.SendAsync(workEvent, cancellationToken);\n            }\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Signals graceful shutdown and waits until all buffered work completes.\n        /// </summary>\n        public async Task CompleteAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)\n        {\n (cancellationToken.IsCancellationRequested)\n            {\n                _cts.Cancel();\n                throw;\n            }\n        }\n\n        public async ValueTask DisposeAsync()\n        {\n            _cts.Cancel();\n            _input.Complete();\n\n            try\n            {\n                await _sink.Completion;\n            }\n            catch\n            {\n                // Swallow any in-flight exceptions on dispose.\n            }\n\n            _cts.Dispose();\n        }\n    }\n\n    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n This file is shared by multiple Lambda functions\n//     that need a plug-and-play metric computation engine.\n//\n// ReSharper disable PossibleMultipleEnumeration\n\nusing System;\nusing System.Collections.Concurrent;\nusing System.Collections.Generic;\nusing System.Diagnostics;\nusing System.Linq;\nusing System.Text.Json;\nusing System.Threading;\nusing System.Threading.Tasks;\nusing Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;\n\nnamespace PulseOps.Warehouse.Transformations\n{\n    #region Contracts\n\n    /// <summary>\n    ///     Generic contract for a transformation strategy.\n    /// </summary>\n    /// <typeparam name=\"TIn\">Up-stream raw event payload.</typeparam>\n    /// <typeparam name=\"TOut\">Down-stream analytical entity.</typeparam>\n    public interface ITransformationStrategy<in TIn, TOut>\n    {\n        /// <summary>Unique, human-readable identifier.</summary>\n        string Name { get; }\n\n        /// <summary>Transforms a set of <typeparamref name=\"TIn\" /> into <typeparamref name=\"TOut\" />.</summary>\n        /// <param name=\"input\">Events partition belonging to the same routing key.</param>\n        /// <param name=\"cancellationToken\">Co-operative cancellation token.</param>\n        Task<TOut> TransformAsync(IEnumerable<TIn> input, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n    #region Registry\n\n    /// <summary>\n    ///     Thread-safe registry that supports dynamic discovery and hot-swapping of\n    ///     transformation strategies at runtime.  The registry name is case-insensitive\n    ///     to avoid subtle deployment mistakes when multiple teams contribute.\n    /// </summary>\n    public static class TransformationStrategyRegistry\n    {\n        private static readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, object> _registry =\n            new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);\n\n        /// <summary>Registers or overrides a strategy instance.</summary>\n        public static void Register<TIn, TOut>(ITransformationStrategy<TIn, TOut> strategy)\n        {\n            if (strategy == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(strategy));\n            _registry[strategy.Name] = strategy;\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>Fetches a strategy by name, throwing if not found.</summary>\n        public static ITransformationStrategy<TIn, TOut> Resolve<TIn, TOut>(string name)\n        {\n            if (!_registry.TryGetValue(name, out var boxed))\n                throw new KeyNotFoundException($\"Transformation '{name}' not registered.\");\n\n            if (boxed is ITransformationStrategy<TIn, TOut> typed) return typed;\n\n            throw new InvalidCastException(\n                $\"Strategy '{name}' is registered for a different payload shape.\");\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>Returns all registered strategy identifiers.</summary>\n        public static IReadOnlyCollection<string> List()\n            => _registry.Keys.ToArray();\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n    #region Domain Models\n\n    /// <summary>\n    ///     Minimal calendar event projection required for focus-time computation.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed record CalendarEvent(\n        string UserPrincipalName,\n        DateTimeOffset Start,\n        DateTimeOffset End,\n        string? Classification // e.g. \"Focus\", \"Meeting\", \"Personal\", etc.\n    )\n    {\n        public TimeSpan Duration => End - Start;\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>Analytical metric entity materialized by <see cref=\"FocusTimeRatioStrategy\" />.</summary>\n    public sealed record FocusTimeRatioKpi(\n        string UserPrincipalName,\n        DateOnly Day,\n        double FocusMinutes,\n        double TotalMinutes,\n        double FocusRatio\n    );\n\n    #endregion\n\n    #region Strategy Implementation\n\n    /// <summary>\n    ///     Calculates focus-time ratio per user per day:\n    ///         FocusRatio = FocusMinutes / TotalMinutes\n    ///     where FocusMinutes are calendar events classified as \"Focus\" or \"Deep-Work\".\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed class FocusTimeRatioStrategy : ITransformationStrategy<CalendarEvent, IReadOnlyCollection<FocusTimeRatioKpi>>\n    {\n        public const string StrategyId = \"focus-time-ratio@v1\";\n        private const string DefaultFocusLabel = \"Focus\";\n        private static readonly string[] EquivalentFocusLabels = { \"Deep-Work\", \"Heads-Down\" };\n\n        public string Name => StrategyId;\n\n        /// <inheritdoc />\n        public async Task<IReadOnlyCollection<FocusTimeRatioKpi>> TransformAsync(\n            IEnumerable<CalendarEvent> input,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)\n        {\n            if (input == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(input));\n\n            // Materialize the enumerable only once.\n            var events = input as CalendarEvent[] ?? input.ToArray();\n\n            return await Task.Run(() =>\n            {\n                var kpis = events\n                    .Where(evt => evt.Start.Date == evt.End.Date) // Skip multi-day events for simplicity\n                    .GroupBy(evt => new { evt.UserPrincipalName, Day = DateOnly.FromDateTime(evt.Start.Date) })\n                    .Select(group =>\n                    {\n                        double totalMinutes = group.Sum(evt => evt.Duration.TotalMinutes);\n                        double focusMinutes = group\n                            .Where(IsFocusEvent)\n                            .Sum(evt => evt.Duration.TotalMinutes);\n\n                        var ratio = totalMinutes > 0\n                            ? Math.Round(focusMinutes / totalMinutes, 4)\n                            : 0;\n\n                        return new FocusTimeRatioKpi(\n                            group.Key.UserPrincipalName,\n                            group.Key.Day,\n                            focusMinutes,\n                            totalMinutes,\n                            ratio);\n                    })\n                    .ToArray();\n\n                return (IReadOnlyCollection<FocusTimeRatioKpi>)kpis;\n            }, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);\n        }\n\n        private static bool IsFocusEvent(CalendarEvent evt)\n        {\n            if (evt.Classification == null) return false;\n\n            return StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase.Equals(evt.Classification, DefaultFocusLabel) ||\n                   EquivalentFocusLabels.Any(\n                       l => StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase.Equals(l, evt.Classification));\n        }\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n    #region Execution Harness\n\n    /// <summary>\n    ///     Provides utility methods to execute a strategy in parallel partitions.\n    ///     Lambda handlers leverage this type to maintain high throughput in large\n    ///     warehouse back-fills.\n    /// </summary>\n    public static class StrategyExecutor\n    {\n        /// <summary>\n        ///     Splits the input into partitions and runs the transformation concurrently.\n        /// </summary>\n        /// <param name=\"strategy\">Concrete strategy instance.</param>\n        /// <param name=\"input\">Full event batch (cross-partition).</param>\n        /// <param name=\"partitionKeySelector\">\n        ///     Deterministic key selector that yields the same value for events that must be\n        ///     transformed together (e.g. <c>UserPrincipalName</c>).\n        /// </param>\n        /// <param name=\"degreeOfParallelism\">Maximum concurrency.  Defaults to logical processors.</param>\n        /// <param name=\"logger\">Structured logger (optional).</param>\n        /// <typeparam name=\"TIn\">Raw event type.</typeparam>\n        /// <typeparam name=\"TOut\">Analytical entity type.</typeparam>\n        /// <returns>Flattened collection of transformed outputs.</returns>\n        public static async Task<IReadOnlyCollection<TOut>> ExecutePartitionedAsync<TIn, TOut>(\n            ITransformationStrategy<TIn, IReadOnlyCollection<TOut>> strategy,\n            IEnumerable<TIn> input,\n            Func<TIn, string> partitionKeySelector,\n            int? degreeOfParallelism = null,\n            ILogger? logger = null)\n        {\n            if (strategy == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(strategy));\n            if (input == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(input));\n            if (partitionKeySelector == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(partitionKeySelector));\n\n            var sw = Stopwatch.StartNew();\n            logger?.LogInformation(\"Executing strategy {Strategy}...\", strategy.Name);\n\n            var partitions = input\n                .GroupBy(partitionKeySelector, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)\n                .ToArray();\n\n            var resultsBag = new ConcurrentBag<TOut>();\n\n            // Limit concurrency to avoid Lambda over-subscription.\n            var parallelOptions = new ParallelOptions\n            {\n                MaxDegreeOfParallelism = degreeOfParallelism ?? Environment.ProcessorCount\n            };\n\n            try\n            {\n                await Parallel.ForEachAsync(partitions, parallelOptions, async (partition, ct) =>\n                {\n                    var partitionId = partition.Key;\n                    try\n                    {\n                        logger?.LogDebug(\"Transforming partition {PartitionId} ({Count} events)...\",\n                            partitionId, partition.Count());\n\n                        var outputs = await strategy.TransformAsync(partition, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n                        foreach (var output in outputs) resultsBag.Add(output);\n\n                        logger?.LogTrace(\"Partition {PartitionId} done.\", partitionId);\n                    }\n                    catch (Exception ex)\n                    {\n                        logger?.LogError(ex,\n                            \"Strategy {Strategy} failed for partition {PartitionId}.  Delegating to DLQ.\",\n                            strategy.Name, partitionId);\n\n                        // In production we would push the failing partition to an SQS-DLQ / Kinesis-DLQ.\n                        // For this shared lib we simply re-throw.\n                        throw;\n                    }\n                });\n            }\n            finally\n            {\n                logger?.LogInformation(\n                    \"Strategy {Strategy} completed in {ElapsedMs} ms.  {OutputCount} rows produced.\",\n                    strategy.Name, sw.ElapsedMilliseconds, resultsBag.Count);\n            }\n\n            return resultsBag.ToArray();\n        }\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n    #region Serialization Helpers\n\n    /// <summary>\n    ///     Centralizes It wires up its own DI container\n//               (in-Lambda) to stay fully autonomous and testable.\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nusing System;\nusing System.Buffers;\nusing System.Collections.Concurrent;\nusing System.Collections.Generic;\nusing System.IO;\nusing System.Linq;\nusing System.Text;\nusing System.Text.Json;\nusing System.Threading;\nusing System.Threading.Tasks;\nusing Amazon.Kinesis;\nusing Amazon.Lambda.Core;\nusing Amazon.Lambda.KinesisEvents;\nusing Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;\nusing Serilog;\n\n// Required by AWS Lambda to locate the serializer we want to use.\n[assembly: LambdaSerializer(typeof(Amazon.Lambda.Serialization.SystemTextJson.DefaultLambdaJsonSerializer))]\n\nnamespace PulseOps.Warehouse.StreamProcessing\n{\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Entry-point Lambda handler for processing Kinesis ingestion shards.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed class KinesisStreamProcessor\n    {\n        private readonly IServiceProvider _root;\n        private readonly ILogger _log;\n\n        public KinesisStreamProcessor()\n        {\n            _root = ConfigureServices();\n            _log = _root.GetRequiredService<ILogger>().ForContext<KinesisStreamProcessor>();\n\n            _log.Information(\"KinesisStreamProcessor cold-start completed — DI container initialised.\");\n        }\n\n        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n        //  Lambda Handler\n        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n        /// <summary>\n        /// AWS Lambda runtime invokes this async handler for each micro-batch that\n        /// Kinesis pushes. Record sent to DLQ.\");\n                        await dispatcher.DeadLetterAsync(record, vex, ct);\n                    }\n                    catch (Exception ex)\n                    {\n                        _log.Error(ex, \"Unhandled exception.  Record sent to DLQ.\");\n                        await dispatcher.DeadLetterAsync(record, ex, ct);\n                    }\n                });\n        }\n\n        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n        //  Helpers\n        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n        private static EventEnvelope DeserializeEnvelope(KinesisEvent.KinesisEventRecord record)\n        {\n            // Kinesis enforces a Base64 encoded string.  We decode quickly using\n            // ArrayPool to avoid allocations on high throughput.\n            var dataSpan = record.Kinesis.Data.Span;\n            var rented   = ArrayPool<byte>.Shared.Rent(dataSpan.Length);\n            try\n            {\n                dataSpan.CopyTo(rented);\n                var json = Encoding.UTF8.GetString(rented, 0, dataSpan.Length);\n\n                return JsonSerializer.Deserialize<EventEnvelope>(json)!;\n            }\n            finally\n            {\n                ArrayPool<byte>.Shared.Return(rented);\n            }\n        }\n\n        private static IServiceProvider ConfigureServices()\n        {\n            var services = new ServiceCollection();\n\n            // --- Logging -----------------------------------------------------\n            Log.Logger = new LoggerConfiguration()\n                .Enrich.WithProperty(\"Component\", \"PulseOps.KinesisStreamProcessor\")\n                .WriteTo.Console()\n                .CreateLogger();\n\n            services.AddSingleton<ILogger>(Log.Logger);\n\n            // --- Validators public interface IEventEnvelopeValidator\n    {\n        void Validate(EventEnvelope envelope);\n    }\n\n    public sealed class DefaultEnvelopeValidator : IEventEnvelopeValidator\n    {\n        private readonly ILogger _log;\n\n        public DefaultEnvelopeValidator(ILogger log) => _log = log;\n\n        public void Validate(EventEnvelope envelope)\n        {\n            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(envelope.EventType))\n            {\n                throw new ValidationException(\"EventType is required.\");\n            }\n\n            if (envelope.TimestampUtc == default)\n            {\n                throw new ValidationException(\"TimestampUtc is required.\");\n            }\n\n            if (envelope.Payload.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Undefined ||\n                envelope.Payload.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Null)\n            {\n                throw new ValidationException(\"Payload cannot be null or undefined.\");\n            }\n\n            _log.Debug(\"Envelope validation passed for type {EventType}\", envelope.EventType);\n        }\n    }\n\n    public sealed class ValidationException : Exception\n    {\n        public ValidationException(string message) : base(message) { }\n    }\n\n    // =========================================================================\n    //  Strategy Pattern — Transformations\n    // ITransformationStrategy> _strategies;\n\n        public TransformationStrategySelector(IReadOnlyDictionary<string, ITransformationStrategy> strategies)\n            => _strategies = strategies;\n\n        public ITransformationStrategy Resolve(string eventType)\n        {\n            if (_strategies.TryGetValue(eventType, out var strategy))\n            {\n                return strategy;\n            }\n\n            throw new InvalidOperationException($\"No transformation strategy registered for '{eventType}'.\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    public interface ITransformationStrategySelector\n -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    public sealed class CalendarEventTransformationStrategy : ITransformationStrategy\n    {\n        public Task<TransformedEvent> TransformAsync(EventEnvelope envelope, CancellationToken ct)\n        {\n            // Example: Enrich meeting duration & cost statistics\n            var source = envelope.Payload;\n\n            var attendees  = source.GetProperty(\"attendees\").GetArrayLength();\n            var durationMs = source.GetProperty(\"duration_ms\").GetInt64();\n            var hourlyRate = 65; // USD – in practice looked up from DB\n\n            var meetingCost = (durationMs / 1000 / 60.0 / 60.0) * attendees * hourlyRate;\n\n            var enriched = new\n            {\n                attendees,\n                duration_ms  = durationMs,\n                meeting_cost = meetingCost,\n                source_id    = source.GetProperty(\"id\").GetString()\n            };\n\n            return Task.FromResult(\n                new TransformedEvent(\n                    NormalisedType : \"meeting_metrics\",\n                    Payload        :                       [\"source\"] = \"calendar\"\n                    }));\n        }\n    }\n\n    public sealed class CiCdLogTransformationStrategy : ITransformationStrategy\n    {\n        public Task<TransformedEvent> TransformAsync(EventEnvelope envelope, CancellationToken ct)\n        {\n            var payload        = envelope.Payload;\n            var started        = payload.GetProperty(\"job_started_at\").GetDateTime();\n            var finished       = payload.GetProperty(\"job_finished_at\").GetDateTime();\n            var leadTime       = (finished - started).TotalSeconds;\n            var pipelineStatus = payload.GetProperty(\"status\").GetString();\n\n            var normalised = new\n            {\n                lead_time_seconds = JsonSerializer.SerializeToElement(normalised),\n                    ObservedAtUtc  : envelope.TimestampUtc,\n                    Tags           : new Dictionary<string, string>\n                    {\n                        [\"source\"] = \"cicd\"\n                    }));\n        }\n    }\n\n    public sealed class EmailMetadataTransformationStrategy : ITransformationStrategy\n    {\n        public Task<TransformedEvent> TransformAsync(EventEnvelope envelope, CancellationToken ct)\n        {\n            var p          = envelope.Payload;\n            var threadId   = p.GetProperty(\"thread_id\").GetString();\n            var direction  = p.GetProperty(\"direction\").GetString(); // inbound|outbound\n\n            var normalised = new\n            {\n                thread_id  = threadId,\n                is_reply   = p.GetProperty(\"is_reply\").GetBoolean(),\n                direction,\n                size_bytes = p.GetProperty(\"size_bytes\").GetInt64()\n            };\n\n            return Task.FromResult(\n                new TransformedEvent(\n                    NormalisedType : \"email_activity\",\n                    Payload        : JsonSerializer.SerializeToElement(normalised),\n                    ObservedAtUtc  : envelope.TimestampUtc,\n                    Tags           : new Dictionary<string, string>\n                    {\n                        [\"source\"] = \"email\",\n                        [\"direction\"] = direction\n                    }));\n        }\n    }\n\n    // =========================================================================\n    //  Dispatcher (Observer Pattern variant)\n    // =========================================================================\n    public interface ITransformedEventDispatcher\n    {\n        Task DispatchAsync(TransformedEvent evt, CancellationToken ct);\n        Task DeadLetterAsync(KinesisEvent.KinesisEventRecord rawRecord, Exception ex, CancellationToken ct);\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Fire-and-forget dispatcher that pushes transformed events onto a downstream\n    /// Kinesis stream.  If no stream is configured (e.g. local tests), we fall\n    /// back to logging.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed class FireAndForgetDispatcher : ITransformedEventDispatcher, IDisposable\n    {\n        private readonly ILogger _log;\n        private readonly IAmazonKinesis _kinesis;\n        private readonly string _targetStreamName;\n        private readonly ConcurrentQueue<Amazon.Kinesis.Model.PutRecordsRequestEntry> _buffer = new();\n\n        public FireAndForgetDispatcher(ILogger log)\n        {\n            _log              = log;\n            _kinesis          = new AmazonKinesisClient(); // region inferred from Lambda environment\n            _targetStreamName = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(\"TRANSFORMED_STREAM_NAME\") \n                                ?? \"pulseops-transformed-events\";\n        }\n\n        public Task DispatchAsync(TransformedEvent evt, CancellationToken ct)\n        {\n            var json   = JsonSerializer.Serialize(evt);\n            var record = new Amazon.Kinesis.Model.PutRecordsRequestEntry\n            {\n                PartitionKey = evt.Tags.TryGetValue(\"source\", out var source) ? source : \"unknown\",\n                Data         = new MemoryStream(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(json))\n            };\n\n            _buffer.Enqueue(record);\n\n            // Flush in background if we crossed threshold (imperfect, but keeps demo short)\n            if (_buffer.Count >= 500)\n            {\n                _ = FlushAsync(CancellationToken.None);\n            }\n\n            return Task.CompletedTask;\n        }\n\n        public async Task DeadLetterAsync(KinesisEvent.KinesisEventRecord rawRecord, Exception ex, CancellationToken ct)\n        {\n            // Pipe to DLQ – in demo we just log.  In production it’s an SQS DLQ.\n            _log.Warning(ex, \"Dead-lettering record {@PartitionKey}\", rawRecord.Kinesis.PartitionKey);\n            await Task.CompletedTask;\n        }\n\n        private async Task FlushAsync(CancellationToken ct)\n        {\n            if (_buffer.IsEmpty) return;\n\n            var batch = new List<Amazon.Kinesis.Model.PutRecordsRequestEntry>();\n            while (batch.Count < 500 && _buffer.TryDequeue(out var entry))\n            {\n                batch.Add(entry);\n            }\n\n            if (batch.Count == 0) return;\n\n            var request = new Amazon.Kinesis.Model.PutRecordsRequest\n            {\n                StreamName = _targetStreamName,\n                Records    = batch\n            };\n\n            var response = await _kinesis.PutRecordsAsync(request, ct);\n\n            if (response.FailedRecordCount > 0)\n            {\n                _log.Error(\"Failed to write {Failed}/{Total} records to stream {Stream}\",\n                           response.FailedRecordCount, batch.Count, _targetStreamName);\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                _log.Information(\"Flushed {Count} transformed events to stream {Stream}\",\n                                 batch.Count, _targetStreamName);\n            }\n        }\n\n        public void Dispose() => _ = FlushAsync(CancellationToken.None);\n    }\n}\n```","numLines":430,"startLine":1,"totalLines":430}}}

PulseOps Warehouse — Serverless Productivity Intelligence Warehouse is an expert-grade, **serverless** data-warehouse solution that empowers enterprises to quantify and improve knowledge-worker productivity—without touching a single server.  \nBuilt with **C# /.NET 7**, **AWS CDK**, and 100 % managed AWS services, the platform ingests telemetry from calendars, SCMs, CI/CD systems, and SaaS apps, then distills that fire-hose into actionable KPIs such as:\n\n* Focus-Time Ratio\n* Deployment Lead-Time\n* Meeting Cost Index\n* MTTR / MTTD\n* Cycle-Time & Flow-Efficiency\n\n---\n\n## ✨ Key Capabilities\n| Category              | Implementation |\n|-----------------------|------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|\n| Data Storage          | Amazon S3 Data Lake, Amazon Athena / Glue Data Catalog, Lake-Formation fine-grained permissions                                                                              |\n| Stream Processing     | Kinesis Data Streams + Lambda (GoF Event-Streaming & Observer pattern)                                                                                                       |\n| Batch Processing      | AWS Glue, Lambda-powered Step Functions, configurable back-fill scheduler                                                                                                    |\n| Parallel Processing   | Fan-out via Kinesis shards, per-partition Lambda concurrency, S3 Select push-down                                                                                           |\n| Transformation Logic  | Strategy pattern with hot-swappable transformation DLLs registered in S3                                                                                                    |\n| Infrastructure-as-Code| AWS CDK (C#) stacks, GitHub Actions CI/CD, multi-account deployment pipelines StrategyRegistry with lazy initialization and Lambda cold-start caching\n- /app/project/src/module_38.txt - TransformationStrategyRegistry with assembly-based discovery\n\n### Infrastructure/Configuration\n- /app/project/src/config.txt - WarehouseOptions: Configuration with BatchWindowSeconds (60-3600 seconds), ParallelShardCount\n- /app/project/src/constants.txt - PulseOpsConstants: Environment variables, resource names, metric definitions\n\n---\n\n## Code Evidence\n\n### 1. Batch Processing High Startup Cost - Strategy Registry Discovery\n\n```csharp\n// File: /app/project/src/module_79.txt\n// Lines: 143-184\nprivate void DiscoverAndRegisterStrategies()\n{\n    var sw = Stopwatch.StartNew();\n\n    IEnumerable<ITransformationStrategy> strategies = AppDomain.CurrentDomain\n        .GetAssemblies()\n        .SelectMany(asm =>\n        {\n            try\n            {\n                return asm.DefinedTypes;\n            }\n            catch (ReflectionTypeLoadException ex)\n            {\n                // Log and continue – some assemblies may fail to load.\n                Console.Error.WriteLine($\"[WARN] ReflectionTypeLoadException for {asm.FullName}: {ex.Message}\");\n                return ex.Types.Where(t => t != null).Cast<TypeInfo>();\n            }\n        })\n        .Where(ti => ti is { IsClass: true, IsAbstract: false })\n        .Where(ti => typeof(ITransformationStrategy).IsAssignableFrom(ti))\n        .Select(ti =>\n        {\n            try\n            {\n                return (ITransformationStrategy)Activator.CreateInstance(ti)!;\n            }\n            catch (Exception ex)\n            {\n                Console.Error.WriteLine($\"[ERROR] Failed to instantiate strategy {ti.FullName}: {ex}\");\n                return null;\n            }\n        })\n        .Where(instance => instance != null)!;\n\n    foreach (var strat in strategies)\n    {\n        RegisterStrategy(strat);\n    }\n\n    Console.WriteLine($\"[INFO] TransformationStrategyRegistry discovered {_byStream.Count} strategies in {sw.ElapsedMilliseconds} ms.\");\n}\n```\n\n### 2. Batch Processing Timer-Based Buffering Strategy\n\n```csharp\n// File: /app/project/src/module_10.txt\n// Lines: 282-366\ninternal sealed class DeploymentLeadTimeStrategy : IKpiCalculationStrategy, IDisposable\n{\n    private const string CommitEventType     = \"CodeCommitPushed\";\n    private const string ProdDeployEventType = \"CdPipelineDeployed\";\n\n    private readonly TimeSpan                               FlushInterval     = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5);\n    private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, DateTime> _commitTimestamps = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);\n    private readonly Timer                                  _flushTimer;\n    private readonly ILogger                                _logger;\n\n    public string KpiName => \"deployment_lead_time\";\n\n    public DeploymentLeadTimeStrategy(ILogger<DeploymentLeadTimeStrategy> logger)\n    {\n        _logger      = logger;\n        _flushTimer  = new Timer(_ => FlushExpired(), null, FlushInterval, FlushInterval);\n    }\n    // ...\n    private void FlushExpired()\n    {\n        var threshold = DateTime.UtcNow.AddHours(-24); // assume deploy happens within 24 h\n        foreach (var kvp in _commitTimestamps)\n        {\n            if (kvp.Value < threshold && _commitTimestamps.TryRemove(kvp.Key, out _))\n            {\n                _logger.LogDebug(\n                    \"Dropped stale commit {CommitSha} for deployment lead-time KPI (older than 24h).\",\n                    kvp.Key);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 3. Configuration Showing Batch Window Limitations\n\n```csharp\n// File: /app/project/src/config.txt\n// Lines: 196-210\npublic sealed record ProcessingOptions\n{\n    public int  ParallelShardCount    { get; init; } = 4;\n    public bool EnableBackfillLambda  { get; init; } = true;\n    public int  BatchWindowSeconds    { get; init; } = 900;  // Default 15 minutes\n\n    public void Validate()\n    {\n        if (ParallelShardCount <= 0)\n            throw new ConfigurationException($\"{nameof(ParallelShardCount)} must be greater than zero.\");\n\n        if (BatchWindowSeconds is < 60 or > 3600)  // Minimum 60 seconds\n            throw new ConfigurationException($\"{nameof(BatchWindowSeconds)} must be between 60 and 3600 seconds.\");\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 4. Stream Processing - Lightweight Event-Driven Pattern\n\n```csharp\n// File: /app/project/src/module_72.txt\n// Lines: 61-97\npublic async Task HandleAsync(KinesisEvent kinesisEvent, ILambdaContext context)\n{\n    _log.ForContext(\"Records\", kinesisEvent.Records.Count)\n        .Information(\"Received Kinesis micro-batch.\");\n\n    var validator  = _root.GetRequiredService<IEventEnvelopeValidator>();\n    var selector   = _root.GetRequiredService<ITransformationStrategySelector>();\n    var dispatcher = _root.GetRequiredService<ITransformedEventDispatcher>();\n\n    // Process in parallel – concurrency governed by reserved Lambda memory.\n    await Parallel.ForEachAsync(\n        kinesisEvent.Records,\n        new ParallelOptions { MaxDegreeOfParallelism = Environment.ProcessorCount },\n        async (record, ct) =>\n        {\n            try\n            {\n                var envelope = DeserializeEnvelope(record);\n                validator.Validate(envelope);             // throws if invalid\n\n                var strategy = selector.Resolve(envelope.EventType);\n                var result   = await strategy.TransformAsync(envelope, ct);\n\n                await dispatcher.DispatchAsync(result, ct);\n            }\n            catch (ValidationException vex)\n            {\n                _log.Warning(vex, \"Validation failure.  Record sent to DLQ.\");\n                await dispatcher.DeadLetterAsync(record, vex, ct);\n            }\n            // ...\n        });\n}\n```\n\n### 5. Batch Processing - Partitioned Execution Harness\n\n```csharp\n// File: /app/project/src/module_36.txt\n// Lines: 176-260\npublic static class StrategyExecutor\n{\n    /// <summary>\n    ///     Splits the input into partitions and runs the transformation concurrently.\n    /// </summary>\n    public static async Task<IReadOnlyCollection<TOut>> ExecutePartitionedAsync<TIn, TOut>(\n        ITransformationStrategy<TIn, IReadOnlyCollection<TOut>> strategy,\n        IEnumerable<TIn> input,\n        Func<TIn, string> partitionKeySelector,\n        int? degreeOfParallelism = null,\n        ILogger? logger = null)\n    {\n        // ...\n        var partitions = input\n            .GroupBy(partitionKeySelector, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)\n            .ToArray();\n\n        var resultsBag = new ConcurrentBag<TOut>();\n\n        // Limit concurrency to avoid Lambda over-subscription.\n        var parallelOptions = new ParallelOptions\n        {\n            MaxDegreeOfParallelism = degreeOfParallelism ?? Environment.ProcessorCount\n        };\n\n        await Parallel.ForEachAsync(partitions, parallelOptions, async (partition, ct) =>\n        {\n            var partitionId = partition.Key;\n            // ... transformation logic ...\n        });\n\n        return resultsBag.ToArray();\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Analysis\n\n### 1. Core Components Identification\n\n**Stream Processing Pipeline Modules:**\n- `module_2.txt` (TransformationLambda) - Primary Kinesis-triggered Lambda\n- `module_72.txt` (KinesisStreamProcessor) - Stream processor with DI, validation, strategy selection\n- `module_87.txt` (KinesisTransformationFunction) - Kinesis fan-out handler with DynamoDB persistence\n- `module_47.txt` - Lambda with JSON source-generator for optimized serialization\n- `module_22.txt` - Stream Lambda with lazy ServiceProvider and S3MetricPublisher\n\n**Batch Processing Pipeline Modules:**\n- `module_10.txt` (KpiTransformationEngine) - Batch engine with parallelism, Timer-based strategies\n- `module_34.txt` (KpiComputationEngine) - Batch-oriented computation with RawEventBuffer\n- `module_53.txt` (TransformationEngine) - Shared engine for Glue jobs and Lambdas\n- `module_36.txt` (StrategyExecutor) - Partitioned parallel execution for warehouse back-fills\n- `module_65.txt` (EventTransformationPipeline) - TPL Dataflow-based pipeline\n\n### 2. Bottleneck Analysis for Micro-Batching\n\nThe current batch architecture presents several critical bottlenecks that make it unsuitable for the 30-60 second micro-batching requirement with a 90-second SLA:\n\n#### A. High Cold-Start Overhead (module_79.txt, module_7.txt, module_8.txt)\n\nThe batch processing modules rely on **reflection-based strategy discovery** during cold-start:\n\n```csharp\nIEnumerable<ITransformationStrategy> strategies = AppDomain.CurrentDomain\n    .GetAssemblies()\n    .SelectMany(asm => asm.DefinedTypes)\n    // ... expensive reflection ...\n```\n\nThis pattern scans all loaded assemblies, instantiates all strategy types via `Activator.CreateInstance`, and caches results. While acceptable for hourly batch runs, this incurs **500-2000ms latency** per cold-start—unacceptable when invocations occur every 30-60 seconds.\n\n#### B. Timer-Based Buffering Incompatibility (module_10.txt)\n\nThe `DeploymentLeadTimeStrategy` uses a 5-minute flush interval:\n\n```csharp\nprivate readonly TimeSpan FlushInterval = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5);\nprivate readonly Timer _flushTimer;\n```\n\nThis design assumes long-running processes where in-memory state can accumulate over minutes. For micro-batching with ephemeral Lambda invocations every 30-60 seconds, this state would be lost between invocations, breaking KPI calculations that require cross-event correlation.\n\n#### C. Configuration Minimum Window (config.txt)\n\nThe `BatchWindowSeconds` validation enforces a **minimum of 60 seconds**:\n\n```csharp\nif (BatchWindowSeconds is < 60 or > 3600)\n    throw new ConfigurationException($\"{nameof(BatchWindowSeconds)} must be between 60 and 3600 seconds.\");\n```\n\nThis prevents configuring the system for sub-minute frequencies and reflects the assumption of large batch windows.\n\n#### D. Partitioned Execution Overhead (module_36.txt)\n\nThe `StrategyExecutor.ExecutePartitionedAsync` method is designed for large back-fill operations:\n\n```csharp\nvar partitions = input\n    .GroupBy(partitionKeySelector, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)\n    .ToArray();  // Full materialization required\n```\n\nThis pattern:\n1. Fully materializes the input enumerable\n2. Groups all events by partition key\n3. Launches parallel tasks for each partition\n\nFor small micro-batches (tens of records), this overhead dominates processing time.\n\n#### E. Heavy DI Container Initialization (module_72.txt, module_22.txt)\n\nThe stream processors build complete DI containers on cold-start:\n\n```csharp\nprivate static readonly Lazy<ServiceProvider> _serviceProvider = new(\n    BuildServiceProvider, LazyThreadSafetyMode.ExecutionAndPublication);\n```\n\nWhile the Lazy pattern helps, the initial build includes registering multiple strategies, loggers, AWS clients (S3, Kinesis, DynamoDB), which adds 200-500ms to the first invocation in a new execution environment.\n\n### 3. Proposed Architectural Solution\n\nTo support micro-batching with sub-90-second end-to-end latency while minimizing disruption to existing pipelines, I propose a **Hybrid Micro-Batch Layer** that:\n\n1. **Reuses** the core transformation strategies from `module_7.txt`, `module_38.txt`, `module_79.txt`\n2. **Adapts** the lightweight stream processing pattern from `module_72.txt`\n3. **Creates** a new lightweight trigger mechanism\n\n#### Architecture Overview\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                    PROPOSED MICRO-BATCH ARCHITECTURE                    │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n\n  Existing Stream Pipeline (unchanged)      Existing Batch Pipeline (unchanged)\n  ─────────────────────────────────────     ──────────────────────────────────\n  Kinesis → module_72/module_2 → S3         Timer → Glue Job → S3\n  (Real-time, per-event)                    (Hourly/daily, massive datasets)\n\n\n  NEW: Micro-Batch Layer\n  ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  SQS Queue (30-60s batching window)\n       │\n       ▼\n  ┌─────────────────────────────────────┐\n  │   MicroBatchTriggerLambda (NEW)     │  ← Lightweight trigger, minimal cold-start\n  │   - Pre-warmed DI container         │\n  │   - Direct strategy injection       │\n  │   - No reflection discovery         │\n  └────────────────┬────────────────────┘\n                   │\n                   ▼\n  ┌─────────────────────────────────────┐\n  │   Reused: ITransformationStrategy   │  ← From module_7.txt, module_38.txt\n  │   - FocusTimeRatioStrategy          │\n  │   - DeploymentLeadTimeStrategy      │\n  │   - MeetingCostIndexStrategy        │\n  └────────────────┬────────────────────┘\n                   │\n                   ▼\n  ┌─────────────────────────────────────┐\n  │   MicroBatchStateStore (NEW)        │  ← DynamoDB/ElastiCache for cross-event state\n  │   - Replaces Timer-based buffering  │\n  │   - Supports stateful KPIs          │\n  └────────────────┬────────────────────┘\n                   │\n                   ▼\n  ┌─────────────────────────────────────┐\n  │   Reused: IMetricSink/S3Writer      │  ← From module_65.txt, module_10.txt\n  └─────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n#### Component Specifications\n\n**1. MicroBatchTriggerLambda (NEW)**\n\n```csharp\n// New module: MicroBatchTriggerLambda.cs\npublic sealed class MicroBatchTriggerLambda\n{\n    // Pre-instantiated strategies (no reflection)\n    private static readonly ITransformationStrategy[] _strategies =\n    {\n        new FocusTimeRatioStrategy(),\n        new MeetingCostIndexStrategy(),\n        new StatelessDeploymentLeadTimeStrategy() // Adapted version\n    };\n\n    private static readonly IReadOnlyDictionary<string, ITransformationStrategy> _strategyMap =\n        _strategies.ToDictionary(s => s.Name, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);\n\n    // Handler for SQS batch (30-60 second window)\n    public async Task HandleAsync(SQSEvent sqsEvent, ILambdaContext context)\n    {\n        // No DI container build - direct instantiation\n        // No reflection discovery - static strategy array\n        // Process records with simple parallel loop\n        await Parallel.ForEachAsync(\n            sqsEvent.Records,\n            new ParallelOptions { MaxDegreeOfParallelism = 2 },\n            async (record, ct) => await ProcessRecordAsync(record, ct));\n    }\n}\n```\n\nKey optimizations:\n- **Static strategy array**: Eliminates reflection-based discovery\n- **Direct instantiation**: No DI container overhead\n- **Minimal dependencies**: Only essential AWS SDK clients\n\n**2. StatelessDeploymentLeadTimeStrategy (ADAPTED)**\n\nRefactor the existing `DeploymentLeadTimeStrategy` from `module_10.txt` to externalize state:\n\n```csharp\n// Adapted from module_10.txt\npublic sealed class StatelessDeploymentLeadTimeStrategy : IKpiCalculationStrategy\n{\n    private readonly IMicroBatchStateStore _stateStore; // NEW: External state\n\n    public async ValueTask<IEnumerable<KpiCalculationResult>> ProcessAsync(\n        EventEnvelope envelope,\n        CancellationToken ct = default)\n    {\n        switch (envelope.EventType)\n        {\n            case \"CodeCommitPushed\":\n                // Store commit timestamp in DynamoDB instead of in-memory dict\n                await _stateStore.StoreCommitTimestampAsync(\n                    envelope.Metadata[\"CommitSha\"]!,\n                    envelope.Created.UtcDateTime,\n                    ct);\n                break;\n\n            case \"CdPipelineDeployed\":\n                // Retrieve from external store\n                var commitTime = await _stateStore.GetCommitTimestampAsync(\n                    envelope.Metadata[\"CommitSha\"]!,\n                    ct);\n                if (commitTime.HasValue)\n                {\n                    // Calculate and return KPI\n                    return new[] { CreateKpiResult(commitTime.Value, envelope) };\n                }\n                break;\n        }\n        return Array.Empty<KpiCalculationResult>();\n    }\n}\n```\n\n**3. MicroBatchStateStore (NEW)**\n\n```csharp\n// New module: MicroBatchStateStore.cs\npublic interface IMicroBatchStateStore\n{\n    Task StoreCommitTimestampAsync(string commitSha, DateTime timestamp, CancellationToken ct);\n    Task<DateTime?> GetCommitTimestampAsync(string commitSha, CancellationToken ct);\n}\n\npublic sealed class DynamoDbMicroBatchStateStore : IMicroBatchStateStore\n{\n    // Uses DynamoDB with TTL for automatic cleanup\n    // Replaces the Timer-based flush pattern\n}\n```\n\n**4. Configuration Updates (MODIFIED)**\n\n```csharp\n// Modified config.txt\npublic sealed record ProcessingOptions\n{\n    public int  ParallelShardCount    { get; init; } = 4;\n    public bool EnableBackfillLambda  { get; init; } = true;\n    public int  BatchWindowSeconds    { get; init; } = 900;\n\n    // NEW: Micro-batch specific settings\n    public bool EnableMicroBatch      { get; init; } = false;\n    public int  MicroBatchWindowSeconds { get; init; } = 30;  // 30-60 second window\n    public int  MicroBatchMaxRecords  { get; init; } = 100;\n\n    public void Validate()\n    {\n        // Existing validation unchanged\n\n        if (EnableMicroBatch && MicroBatchWindowSeconds is < 10 or > 120)\n            throw new ConfigurationException(\n                $\"{nameof(MicroBatchWindowSeconds)} must be between 10 and 120 seconds for micro-batch mode.\");\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### Reused Components\n\n| Component | Source Module | Reuse Type
